# Skills · F · part 28

839 entries, folding-scheme-auditor to forecasting-reverso. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **folding-scheme-auditor**: Audit folding scheme and IVC implementations for accumulator soundness, step circuit binding, cycle-of-curves correctness, and running instance completeness: relaxed R1CS/CCS folding equations, what the folding challenge binds, cross-term commitments, base case handling, non-native field encoding, and what the final compressing SNARK verifies. Use when reviewing Nova, SuperNova, HyperNova, ProtoStar/ProtoGalaxy, Sangria-style, or custom folding-based proof systems in Rust. ([Yue-Zhou1/zkcrypto-audit](https://github.com/Yue-Zhou1/zkcrypto-audit/tree/HEAD/plugins/zk-and-vm-auditors/skills/folding-scheme-auditor) · ★3)

For sequence similarity, use uniprot BLAST. For structure prediction, use chai or boltz.
